# KTM 350 SX-F 2023: aftermarket parts

4042 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 515 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 906 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2621 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
